### S144-U-195 - a Timeline kill row puts the Replay minimap on the kill's own millisecond (s144 uiux cycle 185)

- Row: the U-193 FUTURE "a ribbon seek uses `clock_s * 1000`, which lands before a kill's own ms (230000 < 230254), so at the seek moment the victim is not yet dead on the map - live behaviour too, not a fixture artefact".
- Before (measured, the pure model `web/js/panels/replay_minimap.js` over the ui_mock SR win, and headless Chromium at 1920x1080): `/api/replay/events` served only `clock_s` (timestamp_ms // 1000), the ribbon row carried only `data-clock`, and `_replaySeekToClock` (`web/js/panels/dev.js`) set the map to `clock_s * 1000`. The minimap opens a death window at the kill's own `timestamp_ms`, so for all 5 kill rows the map showed the victim alive (no death cross) and the killer at its last frame sample instead of at the kill spot; at the exact ms the model has the victim dead and the killer on its kill sample, all 5.
- Ruling: the route serves each event's own ms as an additive `timestamp_ms` (floored like `clock_s`; null where the stored timestamp is NULL; `_CACHE_SCHEMA` bumped to `v3-timestamp-ms` per its own rule). The ribbon row carries it as `data-ms`, only when it agrees with the row's shown clock (`floor(ms / 1000) == clock_s`); `_seekFromRow` passes it and `_replaySeekToClock(clockS, matchId, tMs)` puts the map (and the nearest-frame pick) on that moment. A row without one (a you-flagged pin, a NULL timestamp, the live route until RC restarts with this code, the ui_mock non-kill rows) seeks its clock as before. The canvas carries `data-t-ms`, the moment it shows (its caption clock reads whole seconds only). The "Map at m:ss" caption is unchanged.
- Alternatives: (a) seek to `clock_s * 1000 + 999` with no route change - rejected, it shows every event up to 999 ms late (a later event in the same second can already have happened) and still guesses; (b) look the kill up in the match reply's `kills` by second, killer and victim - rejected, it fixes CHAMPION_KILL only and depends on the two routes agreeing on ids, where the exact ms is one field the events table already stores.
- Fixture: the 5 SR CHAMPION_KILL ribbon events in `web/data/ui_mock/replay.json` carry their `kills` row's real `timestamp_ms` (the values U-193 copied from the store); the other ribbon events keep `clock_s` only, so the no-ms path stays covered under ui_mock.
- Guards: `tests/test_routes_replay_events_exact_ms.py` (4: every event's int ms with `clock_s == ms // 1000`; NULL -> null and clock 0; a REAL timestamp floored; ASCII) and `tests/snapshot_panels/test_replay_kill_seek_exact_ms.py` (5: fixture kill events carry their kill row's ms; every row's `data-ms` matches its event; a click or Enter on each of the 5 kill rows puts the canvas on the kill's ms, the pure model has the victim dead at the kill's pos there, the caption reads the row's m:ss and the slider the nearest frame; a row with `data-ms` removed seeks `clock_s * 1000`, the victim is alive there and the canvas digest differs from the exact seek; ASCII). Red at HEAD: 6 failed, 3 passed (the two ASCII checks and the data-ms row check, vacuous while no event carried ms). Live-half digests re-captured for `dev.js` and `replay_events.js` (js_panels.txt, only those two rows moved).
- 5-phase audit (headless): STRUCTURE - no CSS or layout change, two data attributes only; TYPOGRAPHY - none changed; HIT-TARGETS - no new control, the ribbon rows and filters unchanged (test_replay_filter_hit_targets.py green); ASCII - 0 non-ASCII, 0 CR in every touched file; HIERARCHY - a kill row now shows the kill it names: the victim's death cross at the kill spot and the killer standing on it.
- MERGER: the route change reaches the live dashboard only after an RC restart (a lane never restarts); until then the live ribbon has no `timestamp_ms` and every row seeks its clock as before.
- FUTURE: the ribbon's ui_mock CHAMPION_KILL events still carry no `pos` (nothing in web/ reads it). A you-flagged pin row seeks its whole-second clock.

History from 480 to 400: the name was squeezed to 36 px, so "Jinx - SR" broke over two lines beside a one-line 173 px timestamp; at 360 / 320 the timestamp broke over three lines and ran 7 to 56 px past the row's content box; at 1201 (two-column card) "Tristana - SR" broke over two lines.
- Ruling: the inline styles become classes (`hmr-name`, `dim hmr-kda`, `dim hmr-time`; the `.dim` class stays, so every `.dim` reader keeps working) and both match lists wrap with `justify-content: flex-end`. The name takes its one-line width before it grows (`flex: 1 1 auto`), so a too-narrow row wraps the TIME, not the name, and a wrapped KDA / time sits on the right edge under the one-line rows' time column. The name is capped `calc(100% - 4rem)` with an auto right margin, so a long name wraps inside itself beside the grade pip; without the cap "RenataGlasc - Ranked Solo" took a line of its own at 320 and justify-content pushed the lone grade pip to the RIGHT edge (caught by the probe on the first cut, now pinned).
- Alternatives: (a) `margin-left: auto` on the time - rejected, on one line it eats the time's 8 px margin (every row moves) and on a wrapped KDA + time line it splits the free space if the KDA carries one too; (b) CSS-only `justify-content: flex-end` with the inline styles kept - rejected, the name keeps its `0%` basis, so History still squeezes it to 36 px and the 320 timestamp still breaks and spills; (c) wrapping grade + name in one element - rejected, it changes the row's child structure that four other guards read (`:scope > .dim`, the grade chip, the result glyph).
- Geometry: from 600 px up every match-row child rect in both views is identical to HEAD except History at 1201, where the Tristana row now wraps its time (71 px tall, name on one line) instead of breaking the name (61 px). Below 600 rows are 42 to 101 px tall (HEAD 42 to 104), every part inside its row.
- Guard: `tests/snapshot_panels/test_match_row_time_right_align.py` (14: Session and History at 1920 / 1201 / 721 / 480 / 400 / 360 / 320): no inline style inside a row; time right edge = row content right; every child inside the content box; grade pip left of and on the first line of the name; KDA and time never break; the name does not break when the row could hold it; no sideways scroll; at 1920 one 42 px line in name / KDA / time order; at 320 at least one wrapped row per view. Red on HEAD: 14 failed. Live-half digests re-captured for main.js and header.css (code change). The name separator is now written as the JS escape `\u00B7` in both renderers (the precommit gate counts a re-worded glyph line as net-new), so main.js carries two fewer live glyphs and renders the same middle dot; the guard pins that the name still reads "champion, dot, mode".
- 5-phase audit (headless, same widths): STRUCTURE - no sideways scroll of either view, every row child inside its row. TYPOGRAPHY - no font property declared or changed. HIT-TARGETS - row min-height 42 unchanged, the rows' focus ring and keyboard wiring unchanged (test_match_row_focus_ring.py green). ASCII - 0 non-ASCII bytes introduced, 0 CR in every touched file. HIERARCHY - DOM order unchanged; the time keeps one right-hand column at every width.
- FUTURE: the Session REPLAYED CHAMPIONS rows (`web/js/main.js` champ list) still carry inline `flex:1` / `margin-left:10px` styles; at 320 they fit, so no defect is filed. At 320 a History row whose name and KDA cannot share a line takes three lines (name, KDA, timestamp), since the 173 px timestamp and the KDA do not fit one 192 px line.

### S144-U-199 - the footer TFT and ARENA mode pills reach AA in every theme (s144 uiux cycle 189)

- Found by a headless contrast sweep of the TFT Active Match fixtures (`?ui_mock=1&mode=tft|tft_live|tft_round`, 1920 px): every view and width was already free of sideways scroll, and the one text under AA was the footer mode pill.
- Defect: `#mode-pill` sits in the footer at 16 px / 700 (`footer .mode-pill`), which is not WCAG large text, so its floor is 4.5:1. The TFT pill painted solid `--bad` and the ARENA pill solid `--advisory` (defined only by hextech and terminal, so the other four themes inherit hextech's #E84057). Measured on the resolved footer surface: 4.44:1 on arcane (the theme a fresh install boots on), 4.48 on moonlit, ARENA 4.49 on ember, hextech 4.53. SR / ARAM / BRAWL / CLIENT were 6.98:1 or better on every theme.
- Ruling: both red pills take the RM-1550 lift every other red text label already uses (`color-mix(in oklab, var(--bad|--advisory) 70%, var(--text))`, as `.lv-mc-result-loss` and `.csv-enemy-wr-slot.is-bad`). After: 6.60 (arcane) to 7.98 (terminal), inside the band of the four other pills (6.98 to 10.12), so the red pill no longer reads as the weakest label in the footer. Borders unchanged.
- Alternatives rejected: (a) a darker footer surface - moves every footer chip and the pill parity guards; (b) a new `--bad-text` token - a second name for the one lift the dashboard already spells inline (twelve rules in header.css, champ_select_view.css and ops_panels.css used the same 70% mix before this change).
- Guard: `tests/snapshot_panels/test_footer_mode_pill_contrast.py` (24: six themes; TFT and ARENA stamped through the real state path from the `tft` / `arena` snapshot fixtures; every one of the six mode tags at AA on the same pill; the lifted pills stay red, red channel ahead of green and blue by 60 or more). Red at HEAD: 8 failed. css_header live-half digest re-captured (live CSS changed, no glyph edited).
- 5-phase audit (headless, 1920): STRUCTURE - colour only, the footer chip parity / floor / status guards green. TYPOGRAPHY - no font property changed (16 px / 700 measured). HIT-TARGETS - the pill is a non-interactive span; no control moved. ASCII - 0 non-ASCII bytes introduced, 0 CR. HIERARCHY - the red pills now sit in the same contrast band as their siblings and stay red.
- FUTURE: ARENA and TFT pills are the same red on hextech, moonlit and arcane (`--advisory` equals `--bad` there), although the rule comment calls arena "pink"; telling the two modes apart by pill colour is a design call, not filed as a defect. Home: `.home-recent-meta` (the RECENT row's mode label, `--text-faint` family) measured 3.64:1 at 1920 on arcane - next candidate.

The 14 px badge and 11 px tier sizes are operator exceptions and were not touched.

### S144-U-208 - the archive Post Game Review's Back button draws the house focus ring, and keyboard focus no longer reads as a mouse hover (s144 uiux cycle 198)

- Taken from the cycle 197 next-candidate row (the `.hpgr-back` hover / focus fill, unmeasured). Measured headless: `#view-historical-pgr` through the real History flow under `?ui_mock=1&theme=<t>` (first session row, first match row), six themes, 1920x920 and 923x1280. A focusable sweep of the view finds ONE control, `#hpgr-back`.
- Text is clean in every state: the label on the hover / focus fill (`--info-soft` over the view surface) 10.66 (arcane) to 13.24 (terminal), at rest 12.92 hextech.
- Defect: `web/css/panels/home.css` gave `.hpgr-back:hover` and `.hpgr-back:focus-visible` one rule with `outline: none`. The keyboard-focused button computed `outline-style: none` and `box-shadow: none` and painted exactly the hover state (fill plus a 1px `--info` border, 7.45 to 8.49 on the surface), so focus could not be told from a pointer resting on it, and the only focus cue was a 1px border. Every other swept control carries the RM-1210 ring.
- Ruling: hover and focus keep the shared fill and border; a separate `.hpgr-back:focus-visible` rule draws the RM-1210 ring, `outline: 2px solid var(--accent)`, offset 2px (the head holds no other control, and the ring reach does not cover the title or the meta line, pinned). An OUTLINE, not the `--focus-ring` box-shadow, as RM-1210. Hover draws no ring (pinned). After: ring against `#view-historical-pgr`'s surface 6.99 (arcane), 8.06 hextech, 8.68 terminal, 9.45 ember, 9.72 bloodmoon, 10.12 moonlit; not clipped by any overflow box at either width.
- Guard `tests/snapshot_panels/test_hpgr_back_focus_ring.py` (12: six themes x two widths). Red at HEAD's CSS: 12 failed (no ring). css_panels live-half digest re-captured for home.css (live CSS changed, no glyph edited).
- 5-phase audit (headless, 1920x920 / 923x1280): STRUCTURE - an outline only, no box size change; the archive view, head font-swap and hidden-display guards green. TYPOGRAPHY - no font property changed. HIT-TARGETS - `.hpgr-back` keeps its 42 px floor and now has a 2px focus ring at 3:1 or better. ASCII - 0 non-ASCII bytes introduced, LF. HIERARCHY - unchanged at rest; the ring appears only under keyboard focus.
- FUTURE: none new from this unit. The hero DEFEAT pill (3.63 arcane, large-text floor only) and the per-theme red / `--accent-2` text token stay open from S144-U-207.

### S144-U-209 - a Build Insights table tab whose fetch fails says its stats are unavailable, not "play a few games", and the next visit fetches again (s144 uiux cycle 199)

- First, the cycle 198 next-candidate row: re-run the RM-1210 focus-ring sweep. Measured headless under `/?ui_mock=1&mode=<sr|tft|aram|arena>` at 1920x920. Every view was swept (home, lobby, champ-select, session, history, user-builds, build-insights, settings, replay, last-match, active-match). Every tab of each view was opened through its real button, and the view menu was opened. Each visible, enabled focusable was focused and checked for an outline of at least 1px or a box-shadow that appears on focus. Result: CLEAN. 0 controls without a ring, out of 2 to 38 per state. Closed; no change.
- Then, the cycle 196 to 198 next-candidate row: the empty-state lines of the Build Insights chart panels. Each tab's fixture was answered by an empty payload, and then by HTTP 500, through route interception. Six themes, 1920x920 and 923x1280, all nine tabs, every visible text line on its resolved surface. Every line met its floor (`.dw-empty` / `.se-empty` / `.pf-empty` / `.op-empty` / `.cb-empty` / `.bi-empty`). The chart tabs tell the two cases apart ("Not enough ..." vs "... stats unavailable.").
- Defect, found by the same probe: the four TABLE tabs (Items, Skills, Runes, Spells) did not tell them apart. `web/js/panels/build_insights.js` `_fetch` ran one `finish(data)` for every outcome. An HTTP error, a network error and an `ok: false` reply all reached it as `null`, set `st.items = []` and `st.loaded = true`, and painted the empty-corpus line. Under a 500 the Items tab read "No item data yet - play a few games (need at least 20 buys per item).", and the other three the same with their own unit. That is a false instruction. The failure also stuck: `loaded` was set and the ui_mock path cached the failed promise, so leaving the tab and coming back never fetched again, until the Min filter changed.
- Ruling: each table tab carries a `failMsg` in the chart tabs' wording ("Item stats unavailable.", "Skill ...", "Rune ...", "Spell ..."). On a failure, `finish` sets `st.failed`, leaves `loaded` false and drops the cached ui_mock promise. `_render` paints `failMsg` before the empty check. A real empty reply (`items: []`) still paints the "No <x> data yet" line. The next activation of the tab fetches again; `renderBuildInsights` runs on view entry only (`web/js/main.js` view switch), so a failing route is retried per visit, not per tick.
- Alternatives rejected: (a) a lowercase "unavailable" like the `renderBuildInsights` catch fallback. The chart tabs on the same view use "<Thing> stats unavailable.", so the view reads one way. (b) Caching the failure, as before. A transient server error would then need a Min-filter edit to clear, which the chart tabs (`_degraded` caches nothing) do not need.
- Guard `tests/snapshot_panels/test_build_insights_table_fetch_failure.py` (12: four tabs x three cases):
  - a 500 renders the tab's unavailable line and no "play a few games";
  - an empty reply keeps the empty line;
  - a failure followed by a restored fixture renders the real table on the next visit, with the route hit again.
  - Red at HEAD's JS: 8 failed (the 4 unavailable cases and the 4 retry cases); the 4 empty cases were green at HEAD.
- js_panels live-half digest re-captured for build_insights.js (live JS changed, no glyph edited).
- 5-phase audit (headless, six themes, 1920x920 / 923x1280, all four table fixtures failed):
  - STRUCTURE: the same `.bi-empty` node; 0 px horizontal overflow; in view at both widths.
  - TYPOGRAPHY: 18 px, the existing `.bi-empty` rule; no CSS changed.
  - HIT-TARGETS: no control added or moved.
  - ASCII: 0 non-ASCII bytes introduced, LF.
  - HIERARCHY: the unavailable line measures 6.98 (hextech) to 8.70 (terminal) on its surface, the same secondary slot as the empty line it replaces.
- FUTURE: the `renderBuildInsights` catch fallback still paints a bare lowercase "unavailable" into the Items mount only, whatever tab is active (unreachable by any measured path; a consistency row).

### S144-U-210 - the Replay match list says its matches did not load when its fetch fails, not that the store is empty, and the next visit fetches again (s144 uiux cycle 200)

- First, the cycle 199 next-candidate row: a failed-fetch sweep of the Home, Session, History, Lobby and Replay views. Each view's fixture (`web/data/ui_mock/<view>.json`) was answered by HTTP 500 through route interception, headless at 1920x920, and the shown view text was read. Session, History and Lobby keep their page-open placeholders ("-", "loading...", "awaiting LCU lobby data feed"), none of which claims the data is absent. Replay did not.
- Defect: `web/js/panels/dev.js` `_replayViewRefresh` mapped an HTTP error reply to `null`, and `null` reached the empty branch: the RECENT MATCHES list read "no matches in rewind_history.db" under a 500, ui_mock and live `/api/replay/matches` alike. That is a false statement about the store. A reply that rejects (a network error, a non-JSON body) only logged, so the list stayed on "loading..." for good. Under `?ui_mock=1` the failed fixture promise was cached, so leaving the view and coming back never fetched again. Two list requests in flight (the view left and re-entered before the first answered) painted in arrival order, so an older failed reply landing last replaced the newer list.
- Ruling: a failed list fetch (HTTP error, rejected fetch, unparsable body, a reply with no `matches` array) paints "matches did not load - reopen Replay to retry", in the same `li.home-empty` slot. The wording follows the S144-U-184 pick-failure line ("could not load <id> - pick it again to retry"): it names what failed and how to retry. `_replayMockLoad` drops its cached promise on a failed load. `_REPLAY.listSeq` lets only the newest list request paint (the S144-U-183 `loadSeq` pattern). A real empty list still reads "no matches in rewind_history.db". `_replayViewRefresh` runs on view entry only (`web/js/main.js` applyView), so a failing route is retried per visit, not per tick.
- Alternatives rejected: (a) a Retry button in the list. It adds a control to audit for one rare state, and the view-entry retry already exists. (b) Treating a 200 with no `matches` key as empty. The route always sends `matches` on success (`dashboard/routes_coach.py` `_serve_replay_matches`), so a missing key is a malformed reply, not an empty store.
- Guard `tests/snapshot_panels/test_replay_list_fetch_failure.py` (9): a 500 says did-not-load (ui_mock, live); a network error and a non-JSON body say did-not-load, not "loading..." (live); a real empty reply keeps the empty-store line (ui_mock, live); a failure then a restored reply renders the fixture rows on the next visit (ui_mock, live); an older failed reply landing after a newer good one leaves the newer rows (live). Red at HEAD's JS: 7 failed; the 2 empty cases were green at HEAD.
- js_panels live-half digest re-captured for dev.js (live JS changed, no glyph edited).
- 5-phase audit (headless, six themes, 1920x920 / 923x1280, list reply failed and empty):
  - STRUCTURE: the same `li.home-empty` node in `#replay-match-list`; 0 px horizontal overflow; in view at both widths.
  - TYPOGRAPHY: 18 px, weight 400, the existing `.home-empty` rule; no CSS changed.
  - HIT-TARGETS: no control added or moved.
  - ASCII: 0 non-ASCII bytes introduced, LF.
  - HIERARCHY: the failure line measures 6.31 (arcane) to 6.94 (terminal) on its surface, the same ratio and slot as the empty line it replaces; it wraps to one more line in the 318 px pane (152 px vs 131 px tall).
- FUTURE: History and Home keep "loading..." for good when their summary fetch fails (History renders nothing on a null reply; Home retries every 20 s but paints nothing). Neither claims absence, so neither is a false statement; a "did not load" line there is a consistency row.

### S144-U-211 - the History SESSIONS list says its sessions did not load when its fetch fails, instead of "loading..." for good or "no sessions in scope", and the next visit or scope pick fetches again (s144 uiux cycle 201)

- The cycle 200 FUTURE row, History half. Measured headless at 1920x920, ui_mock and live `/api/history` alike, with the reply answered by HTTP 500, aborted, or a non-JSON 200 body: the list stayed on the page-open "loading..." for good and the count on "-". A 200 reply with no `sessions` array (the generic `{"ok": false}` answer) read "no sessions in scope", which IS a false statement about the store, so this half was more than a consistency row.
- Defect: `web/js/main.js` `_historyFetchAndRender` returned without painting on a `null` reply and its trailing `.catch(() => {})` swallowed a rejected fetch. `_historyMockLoad` cached a failed fixture promise, so under `?ui_mock=1` leaving the view and coming back never fetched again. Two replies in flight (a scope tab picked before the first answered) painted in arrival order: an older 14d reply landing last painted 14d sessions under the Whole Season tab.
- Ruling: a failed fetch (HTTP error, rejected fetch, unparsable body, a reply with no `sessions` array) paints "sessions did not load - reopen History to retry" in the same `li.home-empty` slot (the S144-U-210 wording), resets the count and the SEASON STATS card to "-", drops the previous scope's sessions, matches, selected session and filter options, and sets the match list to "no sessions loaded" under "SELECT A SESSION", so a failed scope pick never shows the old scope under the new tab. `_historyMockLoad` drops its cached promise on failure; `_HISTORY.fetchSeq` lets only the newest request paint. A real empty scope still reads "no sessions in scope". Retry is per view entry and per scope pick (both already call the fetch).
- Alternatives rejected: (a) keeping the previous scope's rows on a failed scope pick. They would sit under the new scope's tab with the new scope's name, a false reading. (b) Treating a 200 with no `sessions` key as empty. `dashboard/builders.py` `_build_history` always returns `sessions`, so a missing key is a malformed reply, not an empty scope.
- Guard `tests/snapshot_panels/test_history_list_fetch_failure.py` (11): 500 says did-not-load with count "-" (ui_mock, live); aborted, non-JSON and no-sessions replies say did-not-load (live); a real empty scope keeps "no sessions in scope" and "0 sessions" (ui_mock, live); a failure then a restored reply renders the fixture's 14d sessions on the next visit (ui_mock, live); a failed scope pick after a good scope clears the sessions, count, season stats, match rows and detail head (live); an older 14d reply landing after the Whole Season reply leaves the season sessions (live). Red at HEAD's JS: 9 failed, 2 passed (the real-empty cases).
- js_main live-half digest re-captured for main.js (live JS changed, no glyph edited).
- 5-phase audit (headless, default and light themes, 1920 / 720 / 320 x 920, reply failed):
  - STRUCTURE: the same `li.home-empty` nodes in `#history-session-list` and `#history-match-list`; each ends inside its list (right edge equal to the list's), scrollWidth equal to clientWidth, no horizontal page scroll at any width.
  - TYPOGRAPHY: 18 px, weight 400, the existing `.home-empty` rule; no CSS changed.
  - HIT-TARGETS: no control added or moved.
  - ASCII: 0 non-ASCII bytes introduced, LF.
  - HIERARCHY: the failure line sits in the slot "loading..." held, and wraps to one more line at 320 (174 px vs 131 px tall). Contrast not re-measured: the same `.home-empty` class and card as the loading and empty lines it replaces.
- FUTURE: a successful scope pick keeps `_HISTORY.selectedSession` from the previous scope, so with no filter active `_historyApplyFilters` renders the new scope's session at that index in the match list while no session row is marked active. Not measured rendered this cycle; a row.
- FUTURE: the Home half of the cycle 200 row (Home retries every 20 s but paints nothing on a failed summary fetch).

### S144-U-212 - Home says its summary did not load when the /api/home/summary fetch fails, instead of "loading..." under "no games yet today", and a failed mode-tab switch drops the previous tab (s144 uiux cycle 202)

- The cycle 201 FUTURE row (Home half of the cycle 200 row). Measured headless at 1920x920, ui_mock and live `/api/home/summary` alike, with the reply answered by HTTP 500, aborted, or a non-JSON 200 body: RECENT 3 and THIS WEEK stayed on the page-open "loading..." and the hero sub-line on its authored "no games yet today", a false statement about the match store. A 200 reply with no `recent` array (the generic `{"ok": false}` answer) painted "no matches yet" and "no games this week", false too. Under `?ui_mock=1` the failed fixture promise was cached, so the 20 s poll and every later visit re-read the failure and never fetched again. A mode tab picked while the fetch failed kept the previous tab's match cards, Tonight's Pick and chip values under the newly active tab.
- Defect: `web/js/main.js` `_homeFetchAndRender` returned without painting on a `null` reply and its trailing catch swallowed a rejected fetch; `_homeMockLoad` cached a failed promise.
- Ruling: a failed fetch (HTTP error, rejected fetch, unparsable body, a reply with no `recent` array) paints "summary did not load - retrying" on `#home-hero-sub`, "matches did not load - retrying" in RECENT 3 and "week did not load - retrying" in THIS WEEK (the same `.home-empty` slot), resets the KDA / CS chips to "-" and clears their sparklines, hides Tonight's Pick, clears the hero motif, applies the W/L strip's existing empty-tab rule, and re-aims the snapshot card at the active tab. "retrying" is honest: the 20 s poll, a view entry and a tab pick all fetch again (History's "reopen History to retry" is not, since History does not poll). EXCEPTION: when the active tab already painted from a good reply (`_HOME.paintedTab`), a failed refetch keeps that paint - same scope, last good read - so a transient poll blip does not blank a populated Home every 20 s. `_homeMockLoad` drops its cached promise on failure. A real empty store still reads "no matches yet" / "no games this week" / "no games yet today".
- Alternatives rejected: (a) blanking on every failed poll. A 20 s poll would flicker a populated Home to the failure lines on one dropped request, and the painted rows are still the same scope's last good read. (b) Treating a 200 with no `recent` key as empty. `dashboard/builders_home.py` `_build_home_summary` always starts from `{"today": {}, "recent": [], "this_week": []}`, so a missing key is a malformed reply. (c) "reopen Home to retry" (the History wording): Home retries on its own.
- Guard `tests/snapshot_panels/test_home_summary_fetch_failure.py` (10): 500 says did-not-load with "-" chips, no cards, no sparkline nodes, pick hidden (ui_mock, live); aborted, non-JSON and no-recent replies say did-not-load (live); a real empty store keeps the three empty lines (live); a failure then a restored reply renders the fixture's cards on the next visit (ui_mock, live); a failed SR tab after a good ALL paint drops the old cards, pick and chips (live); a failed refetch of the same tab keeps the paint byte-for-byte (live). Red at HEAD's JS: 8 failed, 2 passed (the real-empty and same-tab-keep cases).
- js_main live-half digest re-captured for main.js (live JS changed, no glyph edited).
- 5-phase audit (headless, all six themes at 1920 x 920, hextech at 720 and 320, moonlit at 320, reply failed):
  - STRUCTURE: the same `.home-empty` div in `#home-recent-list` / `#home-week-list` and the existing `#home-hero-sub`; each ends at its host's right edge, scrollWidth equal to clientWidth, document scrollWidth equal to the viewport at every width.
  - TYPOGRAPHY: 18 px, weight 400, the existing `.home-empty` and hero sub rules; no CSS changed.
  - HIT-TARGETS: no control added or moved.
  - ASCII: 0 non-ASCII bytes introduced, LF.
  - HIERARCHY: contrast composited over the stacked surfaces (the S144-U-200 helpers): hero sub 6.37 (hextech) to 8.18 (terminal), card lines 5.52 (arcane) to 6.52 (terminal), all over AA 4.5. The card lines sit in the "loading..." slot at the same 131 px height at 1920 and wrap to 153 px at 320; the hero sub wraps to two lines (44 px) at 320.
- FUTURE: `#home-hero-sub` is authored as "no games yet today" in `web/index.html`, so for the first fetch's round trip the hero claims an empty day before anything loaded. A "loading..." author value is a consistency row.
- FUTURE: the History selectedSession carry-over across scope picks (cycle 201 FUTURE) is still open.

### S144-U-213 - the Session / History match-row wrap rules move into RM-726 slice HS-4 and keep their reach, so the rebased lane passes the CSS split plan (s144 uiux cycle 206)
- Context: the driver's rebase of lane/uiux onto local main conflicted (main's RM-1974 (b) moved the RM-1776 / RM-1780 720 px rules into slices HS-2 / CV-2). This cycle rebased all 38 lane commits: every tests/guard_data/web_live_half conflict was re-captured with `_recapture_live_half()`, and the two header.css conflicts (S144-U-180, S144-U-197) were resolved by re-homing the S144-U-180 PARTY MAINS block after main's moved RM-1776 block and applying S144-U-197's 720 -> 840 px line to the moved RM-1776 and the S144-U-180 blocks.
- Measured after the rebase: tests/test_web_css_split_plan_rm726.py `test_slice_anchors_sit_only_inside_the_slice[HS-4]` failed - `history-match-list` 2 hits and `history-match-row` 6 hits outside HS-4. The S144-U-196 / S144-U-198 wrap rules sat in the Session block (HS-3).
- Ruling: the comment and rules move into HS-4, right after `.history-match-row .dim`, i.e. after the base row rule they override. The list-scoped selectors (`.session-match-list .history-match-row`, `.history-match-list .history-match-row`) would name an HS-3 anchor inside HS-4, so the row selector becomes `.history-match-row:has(> .hmr-name)`: a Session MATCHES row and a History row are exactly the rows with an `.hmr-name` part (web/js/main.js), so the reach and the 0,2,0 / 0,3,0 specificity are unchanged.
- Alternatives rejected: (a) bare `.history-match-row` (the first draft). The verifier REFUTED it: the Session REPLAYED CHAMPIONS rows (`#session-champs`) share the class, so they would have started to wrap and to pack flex-end. (b) Keeping the rules in HS-3 and widening HS-4's range. The split plan's slice boundaries are main's (RM-726), not a lane's.
- Guard: tests/snapshot_panels/test_match_row_time_right_align.py `test_the_wrap_reaches_match_rows_only` (2: 1920, 320): champ rows stay nowrap / normal / dim normal, Session and History match rows wrap / flex-end / dim nowrap. With the bare selector it fails 2 of 2.
- css_header live-half digest re-captured (code moved, no glyph).
- 5-phase audit (headless, ui_mock, Session and History at 1920 and 320): STRUCTURE / HIERARCHY unchanged - the existing match-row geometry test (14 cases, every width) passes on the moved rules; TYPOGRAPHY no rule changed a font property; HIT-TARGETS no control added or moved; ASCII 0 non-ASCII bytes introduced, LF.
- FUTURE: the RM-1132 theme-swap inventory (tests/snapshot_panels/test_theme_swap_reads_settled.py `test_inventory_covers_every_theme_swapping_reader`) is red on lane/uiux: nine contrast modules added by S144-U-199 to S144-U-209 have no audit row or runtime pin, and four of them settle with a `wait_for_timeout` sleep.

### S144-U-214 - the nine s144 contrast modules get their RM-1132 audit rows and runtime pins, and four of them read settled values through the transitions-off sheet instead of a sleep (s144 uiux cycle 207)

- Problem (measured): tests/snapshot_panels/test_theme_swap_reads_settled.py `test_inventory_covers_every_theme_swapping_reader` was red on lane/uiux - nine modules (build_insights_chart, footer_mode_pill, historical_pgr, home_muted, home_pick_name, lobby, pgr, session, user_builds `_text_contrast`) stamp `?theme=` and read getComputedStyle with no audit row. Five held `wait_for_timeout` sleeps (400 / 400 / 300 / 250 / 400 ms, plus a 200 ms one after a bare `*` sheet).
- Audit method: each module's own `_measure`, all six themes and every viewport / mode, through a page proxy that lists every transition or animation running anywhere in the page before each getComputedStyle evaluate, with the sleeps turned into no-ops and no sheet; compared value-for-value against a baseline with the sleeps in place.
- Verdicts: footer, Home muted, Lobby, Session - nothing runs at the read (the Lobby Accept / Party re-class transitions only the four border colours, which no read touches). Historical PGR - the archive read is settled without its sleep. Build Insights tabs and Clear button, PGR tabs, Tonight's Pick name, User Builds champion input / Delete / Add - DO transition at the read.
- Ruling: those four openers add the standard transitions-off sheet right after the boot check; every sleep in the nine goes; the historical state-marks read swaps its bare `*` rule for the same sheet. Doctrine (RM-1132): the sheet, not a sleep.
- Found on the way (two silent weakenings): (1) the Build Insights 400 ms sleep was the only wait for the Skills / Runes / Spells / Bench async paint - without it a tab read measured 14 rows ending in "loading..." instead of 83 and the floor tests would still pass on less. Each tab now waits for its own content (`table.bi-table` / `table.cb-table` / the chart marks). (2) The User Builds "+ Add build" read sat after a click with no sleep, so it measured a mid-transition fill even with every sleep in place (hextech 201,172,115 against the settled 205,181,132). With the sheet every read matches the baseline except that one row, which now reads settled.
- Pins: `test_contrast_module_reads_are_settled` (9 modules x 6 themes, the module's own `_measure`, first viewport / mode; footer and Lobby also re-class probed, Lobby held to its border transitions), `test_contrast_modules_hold_their_sheet_and_no_sleep`, and two positive controls (`test_contrast_probe_sees_a_dropped_sheet`, `test_contrast_reclass_probe_sees_a_transition`). Mutation: the sheet line dropped from the PGR and User Builds openers -> 3 failed (both module pins and the source pin).
- 5-phase audit: test-only unit, no page changed - STRUCTURE / TYPOGRAPHY / HIT-TARGETS / HIERARCHY not applicable (no CSS, JS or HTML touched); ASCII 0 non-ASCII bytes, LF, in all seven touched files.

### S144-U-215 - the Active Match BUILD pane at a finished build paints the named build module and says "All six item slots full", instead of the scaffold line "will render here mid-game" for the rest of the game (s144 uiux cycle 208)

- Defect (queue B#3, `docs/qa/UI_UX_QUEUE_2026-07-21.md` In-game overlay; the 2026-07-22 note above re-read it as a "will render mid-game" placeholder). Measured headless under `?ui_mock=1&mode=complete#active-match` (SR, 31:20, six items, `daemon_slayer_picks` [] and `item_build` ""), dashboard and `&overlay=1`: `#am-build-body` held the `web/index.html` scaffold line "DS engine ranks + owned items list will render here mid-game." and no build module.
- Root cause: `_renderAmBuildBody`'s retain-last-good guard (`_shouldRetainBuild`, the 2026-06-29 flicker fix) took `!!build.innerHTML` as "a good render". The static scaffold is content, so whenever the DS rerank, the Meta order and the Ultimate order are all empty (a finished build, or DS :8860 / `/api/build-order` down live) the scaffold was kept for the whole game.
- Ruling 1: only a paint `_renderAmBuildBody` itself stamped (`data-am-build-sig`) is retained (`_amBuildPainted`). A real paint still survives a transient empty tick (pinned in the browser and in node).
- Ruling 2: an empty Daemon Slayer row beside six inventory items read from a liveclient block (`_amOwnedItemIds`: the server `owned_item_ids` drop trinkets and consumables, the raw allPlayers path drops slot >= 6; with no liveclient the count is 0, because the coach `items_display` fallback can carry the trinket) reads "All six item slots full" (`_bmLiveEmptyText`) in the existing, previously unused `_bmEmptyStrip` / `.bm-strip.bm-empty` style, centred in the same 46 px reserved strip height, so nothing below moves. Under six items the row stays a blank reserved strip (a fetch may still land; no stuck "loading..."). Meta / Ultimate empty rows are unchanged (the BATCH A blank-row design). Wording is factual on purpose: six slots can hold components, so not "build complete".
- Found by the audit (MUST-FIX, same slice): `.bm-strip.bm-empty` painted `--text-faint`, 4.31:1 on the BUILD pane surface on arcane (4.39 ember, 4.46 hextech, 4.48 moonlit, 4.52 bloodmoon, 5.03 terminal) for a 16 px / 400 line (floor 4.5). Now `--text-dim`: 6.37 (hextech) to 8.18 (terminal). Colours resolved through a canvas pixel (themes.css surfaces are oklch()).
- 5-phase audit (headless, 1920x920): STRUCTURE - module (Daemon Slayer / Meta Build / Ultimate Build + SHAPER) paints on dashboard and overlay, line one line inside its strip and pane, no document overflow. TYPOGRAPHY - 16 px (--fs-xs), not above its row label. HIT-TARGETS - no new control (text only; the SHAPER steppers are the existing module's). ASCII - 0 non-ASCII, LF, every touched file. HIERARCHY - the cyan bold row label stays the brighter, the italic dim line reads under it.
- Pins: `tests/snapshot_panels/test_ui_mock_active_match_complete.py` (scaffold gone + module + line, dashboard and overlay; SR positive control with icons and no line; six-theme contrast / size / fit / height; retain guard keeps a real paint and drops the scaffold), `web/js/panels/active_match_build_complete.test.mjs`. RM-1132 audit row added (boot-stamped, nothing runs at the read). Mutations: guard reverted -> 9 failed; line dropped -> 8; token reverted -> 4; reserved height dropped -> 6.

### S144-U-216 - the "All six item slots full" line counts only the ids the liveclient block itself reports, so a block whose server could not match the active player no longer borrows the coach's name-resolved items (trinket included) and claims a full inventory at five items (s144 uiux cycle 209)
- Defect (filed by cycle 208's verifier): `_renderAmBuildBody` fed `_bmLiveEmptyText` with `lc ? ownedIds.length : 0`, where `ownedIds` came from `_amOwnedItemIds`. When the summarized liveclient block is present but `owned_item_ids` is `[]` (the server matched no active player, `dashboard/_liveclient.py` `else: owned_item_ids = []`) and the lean roster has no row for them either, `_amOwnedItemIds` fell through to the coach `items_display` names, which can carry the trinket. `lc` was truthy, so five items plus a trinket read "All six item slots full". Reproduced in the browser (retain-guard case `unmatched`) before the fix.
- Ruling: the full-inventory line counts `_amLiveclientOwnedIds(lc)` only - the server `owned_item_ids` first, else the active player's own roster row at slots 0-5, else `[]`. The name fallback still feeds the DS rerank, Meta order and owned badges (unchanged: a lossy owned set beats an empty one there), but never this claim; an undercount shows no line, which is the safe direction. The count joins the build sig (`lo`), so a source change repaints the row even when the display names do not change.
- 5-phase audit (headless, 1920x920): behaviour-only slice, no CSS or DOM shape change. STRUCTURE - module + reserved 46 px strip unchanged on dashboard and overlay (existing pins re-run green). TYPOGRAPHY / contrast - the line's six-theme size / AA / fit pins re-run green. HIT-TARGETS - no control added. ASCII - 0 non-ASCII, LF, every touched file. HIERARCHY - unchanged.
- Pins: `web/js/panels/active_match_build_complete.test.mjs` (server list wins and is trimmed; roster slots 0-5 only, six plus trinket = six, five plus trinket = five; unmatched player = 0; malformed blocks = 0), `tests/snapshot_panels/test_ui_mock_active_match_complete.py` retain-guard case (server six -> line; unmatched with six name ids passed -> no line; roster five plus slot-6 trinket -> no line), `tests/test_active_match_live_fixes.py` source pin follows the split (delegation first, server list before the roster row). Mutations: old count restored -> 1 browser failure; helper forced to six -> 4 node failures.

### S144-U-217 - the THREATS strip strips the Live Client localization key, so a live enemy row shows its portrait and damage-mix donut instead of a hidden portrait beside a grey "?" ring (s144 uiux cycle 210)
- Defect (UI_UX_QUEUE_2026-07-21 In-game overlay: "DS panel THREATS row renders 5 (SR) / 12 (Arena) EMPTY placeholder circles"): the Live Client serves `allPlayers[].rawChampionName` as a localization key, `game_character_displayname_Kaisa` (measured live 2026-07-20; `web/js/panels/aram_balance.js` `_AB_RAW_PREFIX`). `_threatRow` preferred that field and only stripped non-alphanumerics, so every live enemy asked for `/img/champion/gamecharacterdisplaynameKaisa.png` (404, CDN retry 404, portrait hidden) and `/api/damage-mix?champ_id=gamecharacterdisplaynameKaisa` (unresolvable, 400, "?" tile). `_amDsSyntheticCs` preferred the same field, so every live enemy resolved to championId 0 and the relscore / matchup roster was empty. The spike-curve and draft-elo strips read championName first, which misses Wukong (MonkeyKing) and Nunu & Willump (Nunu); both strips need all ten champions to draw.
- Why no test saw it: every fixture (`web/data/ui_mock/active_match_*.json`, `active_match_dscluster.test.mjs`) sets `rawChampionName` equal to the DDragon id, never the live key form.
- Ruling: one resolver pair in `active_match.js`. `_amLiveChampKey(pl)` tries the STRIPPED key first (it is the DDragon id, and carries the id for display names that differ), then championName; `_amLiveChampSlug(pl)` returns the index's DDragon id, else the stripped key, else the display name stripped to ASCII alphanumerics. The THREATS label stays the game's display name. Used at the threat row, `_amDsSyntheticCs`, `_renderSpikeCurveFromCtx` and `_renderDraftEloFromCtx`. The three sites that send NAMES to a server (`_extractBpEnemies`, its scores twin `_extractBpEnemyStats`, capability-gap) prefer championName already and are unchanged; the fixtures are unchanged (the snapshot serves the live shape through a route instead, so existing captures keep their bytes).
- 5-phase audit (headless Chromium, 1920x1080 and 1920x920, live-shaped roster): STRUCTURE - 5 rows, each 28 x 28 portrait (display block, loaded) + 28 x 28 donut, no horizontal overflow (#am-build-body scrollWidth == clientWidth). TYPOGRAPHY - "THREATS" eyebrow computes 16px (--fs-xs floor); the donut "?" sigil keeps its documented operator exception. HIT-TARGETS - the strip is not interactive (no handler, tabIndex -1), no control added. ASCII - 0 non-ASCII, LF, every touched file. HIERARCHY - the portraits now paint where they were hidden. FUTURE (SHOULD-FIX, pre-existing, not this slice): on the full-page Active Match view the strip's bottom edge measures 1093 px at a 1080 viewport (1013 at 920), i.e. it sits at or below the fold; the in-game overlay dock is its primary surface.
- Pins: `web/js/panels/active_match_live_champ_names.test.mjs` (key form, Wukong / Nunu, mock form, junk rows, slug before / after the index loads, the threat row's portrait src / alt and donut champ_id through the real render path, `_amDsSyntheticCs` their_team keys), `tests/snapshot_panels/test_threat_strip_live_names.py` (the SR fixture served with the live name shape; a champion square 404s unless it is a real DDragon id, the CDN is refused, /api/damage-mix answers only a real id; asserts 5 visible loaded portraits, 5 drawn donuts, the asked ids). Mutation: pre-fix `active_match.js` -> the snapshot fails on the first row (`gamecharacterdisplaynameTeemo.png`, display none); the node file fails 9 of 9.
